2026-11-02T02:40:29.7344890Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 22 with the Decode greedy gate replaying its endurance graphs outside CUDA capture

Run 41359713062 (Slurm 28179, COMPLETED 12:44, READY 478 s) adopted P3
on all 12 GPUs under the 84-cell rule and served it. Its Decode greedy
gate launched this time but all four ranks failed after 10 s with
"Cannot prepare for replay during capturing stage". The first case had
matched control bit for bit.

Cause and fix (by Codex, first committed as af54f78 on
perf/pp4-r22-p3dec-20261102 and carried here unchanged so that the
push starts serving): the endurance step captured a graph that itself
called candidate.replay(); PyTorch's replay runs the CUDA generator's
replay prologue, which refuses during capture even when no random
numbers are used. Only the comparison is now captured in its own graph,
and the candidate and comparison graphs are replayed in order outside
capture. Control and candidate keep separate graphs and output
storage; the 10,000-replay endurance and its sticky mismatch flag stay.
Three CPU regressions cover the rejected nesting, no prepare or replay
during capture, separate graphs and a transient mismatch.

Harness, tests and the round-22 note only; kernels and SGLang patches
are unchanged, so this reuses image c7804efb without a build.

Validation: Codex ran the full suite CI-style (1491 passed, 90 skipped,
exit 0) and ruff over the CI paths on this exact tree.
